Firstly, we rewrite
Err(x,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))
, given by
(\ref{LMERR1})
, as
6
6

6

The difference between
(\ref{LMERR1})
and
(\ref{SKIS})
is merely technical. We keep the first term on the right hand side of (
5.18
) with the help of the Taylor expansion of
Hess\,N^{k}(\cdots)
, then we split
Hess\,N^{k}
into
Hess\,N
and
Hess\,\left(N^{k}-N\right)
.

(5.23)

\displaystyle Err(x,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)):=

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{0}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}-\Phi_{0}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\Bigg[\underset{y}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+s(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(y,z_{j})\,ds\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\Bigg]

\displaystyle\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle k^{2}\;\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)(y-z_{j})^{\perp}\cdot\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}},z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})\cdot(y-z_{j})dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle k^{2}\;\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)(y-z_{j})^{\perp}\cdot

\displaystyle\left[\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\bm{d}}\left(\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+\rho\,s(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})^{[1]}\,d\rho\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})\,ds\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot(y-z_{j})dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy.

Secondly, for the shortness reason, we denote

(5.24)

Err(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))=\sum_{\ell=1}^{5}J_{\ell}(\cdot)

and we compute an estimation of the
\mathbb{L}^{2}
-norm of each
J_{\ell}(\cdot),\;\ell=1,\cdots,5
. For this, we have:

(i)

Estimation of

J_{1}(x):=-k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{0}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy.

For simplicity, we set
\beta_{j}
, a scalar function, to be an arbitrary component of the vector field
\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)
. We also set
J_{1,j}
the corresponding
j
th component of
J_{1}
. Then,

\displaystyle J_{1,j}(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{0}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot(y-z_{j})\beta_{j}(y)\;dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{0}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot(y-z_{j})\beta_{j}(y)\;\chi_{D_{j}}(y)\;dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-k^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{D_{j}}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{0}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\cdot(y-z_{j})\beta_{j}(y)\chi_{D_{j}}(y)\;dy\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-k^{2}\int_{0}^{1}Hess\,N_{D_{j}}\left((\cdot-z_{j})\beta_{j}(\cdot)\chi_{D_{j}}(\cdot)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}))\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt.

Scaling to
B
, we obtain

\tilde{J_{1,j}}(x)=-k^{2}\,a^{5}\,\int_{0}^{1}Hess\,N_{B}\left(\cdot\tilde{\beta_{j}}(\cdot)\chi_{B}(\cdot)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t\,a\,x)\cdot x\,dt.

Now, taking the norm on the both sides, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{J_{1,j}}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{5}\;\left\|\int_{0}^{1}Hess\,N_{B}\left(\cdot\tilde{\beta_{j}}(\cdot)\chi_{B}(\cdot)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+ta\,\cdot)\,dt\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{5}\;\int_{0}^{1}\left\|Hess\,N_{B}\left(\cdot\tilde{\beta_{j}}(\cdot)\chi_{B}(\cdot)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t\,a\,\cdot)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}\,dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{5}\;\left\|Hess\,N_{B}\left(\cdot\tilde{\beta_{j}}(\cdot)\chi_{B}(\cdot)\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Due to Theorem 9.9, formula 9.28, page 230 of
[
24
]
, we have

\left\|Hess\,N_{B}\left(\cdot\tilde{\beta_{j}}(\cdot)\chi_{B}(\cdot)\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}=\left\|\cdot\tilde{\beta_{j}}(\cdot)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}.

This implies,

\left\|\tilde{J_{1,j}}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}\lesssim a^{5}\;\left\|\tilde{\beta_{j}}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)},

Since
\beta_{j}
is chosen as an arbitrarily component, we deduce that

(5.25)

\left\|\tilde{J_{1}}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{5}\;\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}\right).

(ii)

Estimation of

J_{2}(x):=-k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}-\Phi_{0}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy.

Recall the expansion
(\ref{expansion-of-Hess})
. Then we have

{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}-\Phi_{0}\right)(x,y)=-\frac{k^{2}}{2}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\,I-i\frac{k^{3}}{24\pi}I_{3}+\frac{k^{2}}{2}\,\Phi_{0}(x,y)\,\frac{A(x,y)}{\|x-y\|^{2}}+\frac{1}{4\pi}\;\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\;\underset{x}{Hess}\left(\left\|x-y\right\|^{n}\right).

Using this we deduce that the dominant term of
J_{2}(x)
is

J_{2}(x)\sim\frac{k^{4}}{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\Phi_{0}(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),y)\,dt(x-z_{j_{0}})\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy.

By taking the norm, we get

(5.26)

\displaystyle\left\|J_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\Phi_{0}(z_{j_{0}}+t(\cdot-z_{j_{0}}),y)\,dt(\cdot-z_{j_{0}})\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{5}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-1}\;\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}.

(iii)

Estimation of

\displaystyle J_{3}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle k^{2}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\Bigg[\underset{y}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+s(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(y,z_{j})\,ds

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\Bigg]\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy

(5.27)

\displaystyle\left\|J_{3}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\Bigg[\underset{y}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(\cdot-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+s(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(y,z_{j})\,ds

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot(\cdot-z_{j_{0}})\,dt\Bigg]\cdot(y-z_{j})\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy\Bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{6}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-4}\;\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}.

(iv)

Estimation of

\displaystyle J_{4}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle k^{2}\;\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)(y-z_{j})^{\perp}\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}},z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})\cdot(y-z_{j})dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy.

Taking the
\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})-
norm on the both sides, we obtain that

(5.28)

\left\|J_{4}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}\lesssim a^{6}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-4}\;\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}.

(v)

Estimation of

\displaystyle J_{5}:=k^{2}\;\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)(y-z_{j})^{\perp}\cdot

\displaystyle\left[\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\bm{d}}\left(\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+\rho\,s(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})^{[1]}\,d\rho\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})\,ds\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot(y-z_{j})dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy,

\displaystyle\left\|J_{5}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}\lesssim\Bigg\|\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)(y-z_{j})^{\perp}\cdot

\displaystyle\left[\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\bm{d}}\left(\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+\rho\,s(\cdot-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot(\cdot-z_{j_{0}})^{[1]}\,d\rho\cdot\mathcal{P}(\cdot,z_{j_{0}})\,ds\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot(y-z_{j})dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\;dy\Bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}

(5.29)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim a^{7}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-5}\;\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}.

Therefore, after combining with
(\ref{CJ1}),(\ref{CJ2}),(\ref{CJ3}),(\ref{CJ4}),(\ref{CJ5})
and
(\ref{J1+...+J5})
, we obtain that

(5.30)

\left\|\widetilde{Err}(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}=\mathcal{O}\left(\left(a^{10}\,d^{-2}_{j,j_{0}}+a^{12}\,d^{-8}_{j,j_{0}}\right)\;\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}\right).

(b)

Computing
\left\|\widetilde{Err}(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}
.

Using the definition of
Err(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))
, see
(\ref{LMERR3})
, we divide it into two parts

Err(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))=J_{6}+J_{7}.

(i)

Estimation of

\displaystyle J_{6}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j})\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot\int_{D_{j}}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle\left\|J_{6}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(\cdot-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j})\cdot\mathcal{P}(\cdot,z_{j_{0}})\,dt\cdot\int_{D_{j}}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{4}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-4}\;\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}.

(ii)

Estimation of

\displaystyle J_{7}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+s(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})ds\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\mathcal{P}(y,z_{j})dt\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle\left\|J_{7}\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\underset{y}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon\right)\right)(z_{j_{0}}+s(\cdot-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(\cdot,z_{j_{0}})ds\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\mathcal{P}(y,z_{j})dt\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\Bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{5}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-5}\;\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}.

Clearly,

\left\|Err(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j_{0}})}=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{8}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-8}\;\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(D_{j})}\right),

and after scaling, we obtain that

(5.31)

\left\|\widetilde{Err}(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{8}\;\left|z_{j}-z_{j_{0}}\right|^{-8}\;\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}\right).

Going back to
(\ref{DFD50})
and using the estimations
(\ref{HN1})
and
(\ref{HN2})
, we can derive that

(5.32)

\displaystyle|I|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{-2h}\;\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left\|\widetilde{Err}(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}+a^{-2h}\;\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\,\left\|\widetilde{Err}(\cdot,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{10-2h}\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{d^{2}_{j,j_{0}}}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{12-2h}\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{d^{8}_{j,j_{0}}}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{8-2h}\;\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{d^{8}_{j,j_{0}}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(a^{10-2h}\,d^{-3}+a^{12-2h}d^{-8}\right)\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{8-2h}d^{-8}\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

We also need to estimate

\displaystyle I^{\star}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\,\frac{1}{\left|1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{(1)}\right|^{2}}\,\Bigg[\sum_{k=1\atop k\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\,\left|\langle\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},k,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{k}^{T}\right))+\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},k,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{k}^{T}\right)),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\;\qquad\qquad\;\qquad\qquad\cdot\sum_{i>k\atop i\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},i,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{i}^{T}\right))+\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},i,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{i}^{T}\right)),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|\Bigg]

\displaystyle\left|I^{\star}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{-2h}\;\Bigg[\sum_{k=1\atop k\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left(\left\|\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},k,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{k}^{T}\right))\right\|+\left\|\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},k,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{k}^{T}\right))\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\cdot\sum_{i>k\atop i\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left(\left\|\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},i,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{i}^{T}\right))\right\|+\left\|\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},i,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{i}^{T}\right))\right\|\right)\Bigg].

By using
(\ref{HN1})
and
(\ref{HN2})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|I^{\star}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{-2h}\;\Bigg[\sum_{k=1\atop k\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left(a^{5}\,d^{-1}_{k,j_{0}}\,\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{k}^{T}\right)\right\|+a^{6}\,d^{-4}_{k,j_{0}}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{k}^{T}\right)\right\|+a^{4}\,d^{-4}_{k,j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{k}^{T}\right)\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\;\cdot\sum_{i>k\atop i\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left(a^{5}\,d^{-1}_{i,j_{0}}\,\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|+a^{6}\,d^{-4}_{i,j_{0}}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|+a^{4}\,d^{-4}_{i,j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{-2h}\;\left(\left(a^{5}\,d^{-3}+a^{6}\,d^{-4}\right)\,\underset{i}{\max}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|+a^{4}\,d^{-4}\underset{i}{\max}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{-2h}\;\left(a^{10}\,d^{-6}\,\underset{i}{\max}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{8}\,d^{-8}\underset{i}{\max}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\right).

Finally,

(5.33)

|I^{\star}|=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{10-2h}\;d^{-6}\;\underset{i}{\max}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{8-2h}\,d^{-8}\underset{i}{\max}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\right).

The analysis of the terms
\underset{n}{\sum}\left|\langle\cdots,R_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}
is more technical. For an arbitrary vector field
F
, we have

\displaystyle\langle F,R_{n}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(\ref{Def-Rn})}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1\mp c_{0}\,a^{h}}{4\pi\,\lambda_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\left(1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{(1)}\right)}\sum_{\ell\geq 1}(-ika)^{\ell+1}\langle F,\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}\int_{B}\;\frac{\left\|\cdot-y\right\|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}e_{n}^{(1)}(y)\,dy\rangle

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{(1)}\right)}\sum_{\ell\geq 1}(-ika)^{\ell+1}\langle\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}(F),\int_{B}\;\frac{\left\|\cdot-y\right\|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}e_{n}^{(1)}(y)\,dy\rangle

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{(1)}\right)}\sum_{\ell\geq 1}(-ika)^{\ell+1}\langle\int_{B}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}(F)(x)\frac{\left\|\cdot-x\right\|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}dx,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle.

Taking the modulus, with the help of the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we obtain that

\left|\langle F,R_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{4}}{\left|1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{(1)}\right|^{2}}\sum_{\ell\geq 1}\left|\langle\int_{B}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}(F)(x)\frac{\left\|\cdot-x\right\|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}dx,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\left|\langle F,R_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{4-2h}\,\sum_{\ell\geq 1}\,\left\|\int_{B}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}(F)(x)\frac{\left\|\cdot-x\right\|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}dx\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{4-2h}\,\left\|\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}(F)\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}\sum_{\ell\geq 1}\,\int_{B}\int_{B}\frac{\left(\left\|y-x\right\|^{2}\right)^{\ell}}{((\ell+1)!)^{2}}dx\,dy,

where we have the convergence of the series
\underset{\ell\geq 1}{\sum}\,\int_{B}\int_{B}\frac{\left(\left\|y-x\right\|^{2}\right)^{\ell}}{((\ell+1)!)^{2}}dx\,dy.

Since we are approaching an eigenvalue of the first family, the dominant part of
\left\|\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}(F)\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}
is
\left\|\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(F\right)\right)\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}
and then

\sum_{n}\left|\langle F,R_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}\lesssim a^{4-2h}\;\left\|\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(F\right)\right)\right\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(B)}\simeq a^{4-2h}\;\sum_{\ell}\frac{\left|\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(F\right),e_{\ell}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}}{\left|1-k^{2}\,\eta\,\lambda_{\ell}^{(1)}\,a^{2}\right|^{2}}\simeq a^{4-4h}\;\left|\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(F\right),e_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

Finally,

(5.34)

\sum_{n}\left|\langle F,R_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{4-4h}\;\left|\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(F\right),e_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}\right).

Remark that when
F
or
\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(F\right)
is a constant vector field, using the representation
e_{\ell}^{(1)}=Curl(\phi_{\ell}),\;\mathrm{div}\,(\phi_{\ell})=0,\nu\times\phi_{\ell}=0
and integration by parts, the series
\underset{\ell}{\sum}\left|1-k^{2}\,\eta\,\lambda_{\ell}^{(1)}\,a^{2}\right|^{-2}\left|\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(F\right),e_{\ell}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}

is vanishing. In this case, consequently,
\underset{n}{\sum}\left|\langle F,R_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}
will be zero.

By utilizing

takes the following form

(5.35)

\displaystyle\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\left|\langle\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}},e_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}}{\left|1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\right|^{2}}+\sum_{n\neq n_{0}}\,\left|\langle\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}},e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{4-4h}\left|\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{6-2h}\,d^{-6}\;\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+\,a^{4-2h}d^{-8}\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{-4h}\,\aleph\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))+\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\right|^{2}.

For the last term, we know that

\displaystyle\left|\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))+\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))\right),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \left|\langle\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right))+\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \left|\langle\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\right|^{2}+\left|\langle\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\right|^{2},

Then there holds

\displaystyle\left|\langle\widetilde{Err}(x,j_{0},j,\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\ a^{10}\left\lvert\int_{B}e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}(x)\int_{B}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{ka}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+tax,z_{j})\cdot x\,dt\cdot y\cdot\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\,dx\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ a^{10}\int_{B}\left\lvert\int_{B}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{ka}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+tax,z_{j})\cdot x\,dt\cdot y\cdot\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\right\rvert^{2}\,dx

(5.36)

\displaystyle\ a^{10}\frac{1}{\left|z_{j_{0}}-z_{j}\right|^{6}}\left\lVert\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\rVert^{2}.

Similarly, from (

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ a^{8}\frac{1}{|z_{j_{0}}-z_{j}|^{8}}\left\lVert\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\rVert^{2}.

ii)

Estimation of

\underset{j}{\max}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

For
x\in D_{j_{0}}
, we have

\left(I+\eta\,\underset{x}{\nabla}M^{k}\right)\,\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)(x)+\eta\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\underset{x}{\nabla}M^{k}(E_{j}^{T})(x)-k^{2}\,\eta\,\sum_{j=1}^{\aleph}\,N^{k}(E_{j}^{T})(x)=E^{Inc}_{j_{0}}(x).

Using the integration by parts, we deduce that

\nabla M^{k}\left(\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)\right)=0,\,j=1,\cdots,\aleph
. Then

(5.43)

\displaystyle\left(I+\eta\,\underset{x}{\nabla}M^{k}\right)\,\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right)(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E^{Inc}_{j_{0}}(x)+k^{2}\,\eta\,N^{k}\left(\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right)(x)-\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)(x)+k^{2}\,\eta\,N^{k}\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right)(x)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle k^{2}\,\eta\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\int_{D_{j}}\Phi_{k}(x,y)\,I\cdot\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy+\eta\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\,\int_{D_{j}}\Upsilon_{k}(x,y)\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,

where
\Upsilon_{k}(\cdot,\cdot)
is given by
(\ref{dyadicG})
.
By Taylor expansion near the center
z_{j}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\int_{D_{j}}\Upsilon_{k}(x,y)\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{D_{j}}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon_{k}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j})\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})dt\cdot\int_{D_{j}}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon_{k}\right)(x,z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot\mathcal{P}(y,z_{j})\,dt\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,

and

(5.44)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{k}(x,z_{j})\,I\cdot\int_{D_{j}}\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)dy

The first integral on the right hand side of (
5.44
) is vanishing and the second term by Taylor expansion give us that

\displaystyle\int_{D_{j}}\Phi_{k}(x,y)\,I\cdot\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+h(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\;dh\cdot(y-z_{j})dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)dy.

Plugging this expansion into equation
(\ref{Att-Vienne})
, we can deduce that

\displaystyle\left(I+\eta\,\underset{x}{\nabla}M^{k}\right)\,\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right)(x)=E^{Inc}_{j_{0}}(x)+k^{2}\,\eta\,N^{k}\left(\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right)(x)+k^{2}\,\eta\,N^{k}\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right)(x)-\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)(x)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle k^{2}\,\eta\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\int_{D_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{k}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+h(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j}+t(y-z_{j}))\cdot(x-z_{j_{0}})\,dh\cdot(y-z_{j})dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\eta\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{D_{j}}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy+\eta\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\,\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon_{k}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t(x-z_{j_{0}}),z_{j})\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,z_{j_{0}})dt\cdot\int_{D_{j}}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(E_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left|\langle\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right),e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\Bigg[\left|\langle\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}},e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{2}\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{2}\,\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|\,\sum_{i>j\atop i\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{i})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}\Bigg].

Taking the series with respect to
n
, we can further deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\Bigg[\left\|\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right\|^{2}+a^{2}\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\right|^{2}+\sum_{n}\,\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{2}\,\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\right|\,\sum_{i>j\atop i\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{i})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\right|\Bigg].

Using the fact that
\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\simeq d^{-3}_{j,j_{0}}
, it is easy to get the following upper bound

\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy\right|^{2}\lesssim\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{d^{6}_{j,j_{0}}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\;d^{-6}\;\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2},

and thus

\displaystyle I^{\star\star}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{j})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)dy\right|\sum_{i>j\atop i\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\Upsilon_{k}(z_{j_{0}},z_{i})\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)(y)dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}d^{-3}_{j,j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|\,\sum_{i>j\atop i\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}d^{-3}_{i,j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{i}^{T}\right)\right\|\simeq d^{-6}\;\left|\log(d)\right|^{2}\;\underset{j}{\max}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Therefore,

(5.47)

\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\left\|\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right\|^{2}+\,a^{6}\,\left|\log(d)\right|^{2}\;d^{-6}\;\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\,\sum_{n}\,\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}

Taking the
\underset{j_{0}}{\max}\left(\cdot\right)
on the both sides of (
5.47
), we have

\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right\|^{2}+\,a^{6}\,\left|\log(d)\right|^{2}\;d^{-6}\;\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\,\max_{j_{0}}\sum_{n}\,\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}.

Remark that the coefficient
\;a^{6}\,\left|\log(d)\right|^{2}\,d^{-6}\sim a^{6-6t}\;\left|\log(a)\right|^{2}
is small, then we obtain

(5.48)

\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right\|^{2}+\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\,\max_{j_{0}}\sum_{n}\,\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}.

To finish the estimation of
(\ref{3rd-proj=inc+err})
, we need to estimate
\underset{j_{0}}{\max}\underset{n}{\sum}\,\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}
. For this, taking the squared modulus on the both sides of
(\ref{Error(n,j,j0)})
, we get

\displaystyle\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{4}\sum_{\ell\geq 1}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}+\left|\langle N\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right),e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{2}\,\left|\langle\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}+\left|\langle\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}(y)\right)}{\left\|\cdot-y\right\|^{2}}dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{4}\,\sum_{\ell\geq 1}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}\right)}{(\ell+1)!}\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}+\left\lvert\langle N^{ka}\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j_{0}}\right)\right),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{4}\,\aleph\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\int_{0}^{1}\underset{x}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon_{ka}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+t\,a\,\cdot,z_{j})\cdot\mathcal{P}(x,0)dt\cdot\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{6}\,\aleph\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\int_{B}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\Phi_{ka}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+h\,a\,\cdot,z_{j}+t\,a\,y)\cdot x\,dh\cdot y\,dt\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{4}\,\aleph\,\sum_{j\neq j_{0}}^{\aleph}\left|\langle\int_{B}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{y}{\nabla}\left(\Upsilon_{ka}\right)(z_{j_{0}}+a\cdot,z_{j}+t\,a\,y)\cdot\mathcal{P}(y,0)\,dt\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)(y)\,dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}.

From (
2.9
), we can know that

(5.49)

N^{ka}\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j_{0}}\right)\right)=N\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j_{0}}\right)\right)+\frac{ika}{4\pi}\int_{D}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j_{0}}\right)(y)\,dy+\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 1}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{D}\left\lVert x-y\right\rVert^{n}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j_{0}}\right)(y)\,dy.

It is obvious that in (
5.49
),
N\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j_{0}}\right)\right)
dominates. Thus by
taking the series with respect to
n
index, using the continuity of the Newtonian potential operator, we get
8
8

8

We neglect the estimation of
\underset{n}{\sum}\left|\langle\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}(y)\right)}{\left\|\cdot-y\right\|^{2}}dy,e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

since the singularity of the corresponding kernel is like the one of the Newtonian operator.

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{4}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\sum_{\ell\geq 1}\int_{B}\int_{B}\frac{\left|x-y\right|^{2\,\ell}}{((\ell+1)!)^{2}}\,dy\,dx+\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{4}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\,\sum_{\ell\geq 1}\int_{B}\int_{B}\frac{\left|\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\left|x-y\right|^{\ell}\right)\right|^{2}}{((\ell+1)!)^{2}}\,dy\,dx+a^{4}\,\aleph\,d^{-8}\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6}\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

For the convergence of the two series appearing on the right hand side, we refer to
(\ref{Series1})
and
(\ref{Series2})
. Then,

\sum_{n}\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}\lesssim a^{4}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{4}\,\aleph\,d^{-8}\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6}\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Taking the
\max\left(\cdot\right)
with respect to
j_{0}
index, we obtain that

\max_{j_{0}}\sum_{n}\left|Error(n,j_{0})\right|^{2}\lesssim\max\left(1;a^{4}\,\aleph\,d^{-8}\right)\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Going back to
(\ref{3rd-proj=inc+err})
and plugging the previous result, we can derive that

\displaystyle\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\,\left[\max\left(1;a^{4}\,\aleph\,d^{-8}\right)\max_{j}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}+\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\right].

We assume that
\,\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\,a^{4}\,\aleph\,d^{-8}\,\sim a^{8-8t-s}
is small enough, which is equivalent to the following condition

(5.50)

4-4t-\frac{s}{2}>0.

In
particular, when the distribution of the cluster of the nanoparticles is made over a volume, we have
\aleph\sim d^{-3}
. Therefore, we have
s\leq 3t
. Coupling this with (
5.41
), i.e.
t\leq 1-\frac{h}{3}
, then (
5.50
) is satisfied only if

(5.51)

h>\frac{9}{11}.

Then

\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right\|^{2}+\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\;\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

For fixed
j_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\left|\langle\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}},e^{(1)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}+\sum_{n}\left|\langle\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}},e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\left|\langle\tilde{E}^{Inc}_{j_{0}},Curl\left(\phi_{n}\right)\rangle\right|^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{2}\,a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\left|\langle\tilde{H}^{Inc}_{j_{0}},\phi_{n}\rangle\right|^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(1\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(1\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(1\right).

Then,

(5.52)

\max_{j_{0}}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j_{0}}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\,a^{4}+\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\;\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Now, from
(\ref{MP1<MP3})
and
(\ref{MP3<MP1})
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{2-2h}+a^{4-2h}d^{-8}\left[a^{4}+a^{4}\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{2-2h}+a^{8-2h}d^{-8}+a^{8-2h}d^{-8}\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

If there holds

2-s-6t<0,

then
\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})=a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6}
, which indicates that

\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim a^{2-2h}+a^{8-2h}d^{-8}+a^{14-2h}d^{-14}\aleph\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

From (
5.41
) and (
5.50
), it is direct to verify that
7-7t-h-\frac{s}{2}>0
. Hence, we can derive

(5.53)

\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim a^{2-2h}+a^{8-2h}d^{-8}.

Otherwise, if there holds

2-s-6t\geq 0,

then
(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})=a^{4}
, which indicates that

\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim a^{2-2h}+a^{8-2h}d^{-8}+a^{12-2h}d^{-8}\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}.

By using (
5.41
) and the fact that
t<1
, it is automatically fulfilled that
12-2h-8t>0
and therefore (
5.53
) still holds.

Following a similar argument above, for
\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)
, we can also know from (
5.42
) and (
5.52
) that

\displaystyle\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{4}+a^{4}\cdot\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\,\max_{j}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}_{j}^{T}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{4}+a^{4}\cdot\max(a^{4};a^{6}\,\aleph\,d^{-6})\,\left[a^{2-2h}+a^{4-2h}d^{-8}\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{4}+a^{12-2h}\aleph d^{-6}.

Since the sufficient conditions (
5.40
), (
5.41
) and (
5.50
) give us
t<\frac{5}{7}
, we see that

\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim a^{2-2h}.

Moreover,

(5.54)

\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim\begin{cases}a^{4}&\mbox{if}\quad t<\frac{2}{3},\\
a^{12-2h}\aleph d^{-6}&\mbox{if}\quad\frac{2}{3}\leq t<\frac{5}{7}.\end{cases}

Since
\aleph=O(d^{-3})\sim a^{-3t}
as
d\sim a^{t}
, by recalling that
t\leq 1-\frac{h}{3}
, then for
\frac{9}{11}<h<1
, we have

(5.55)

\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim a^{2-2h}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\max_{j}\left\lVert\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{E}^{T}_{j}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\lesssim a^{3+h},

which completes the proof.

Remark 5.2
.

Recall the sufficient condition
(
5.50
). Particularly, in the regime that

t=1-\frac{h}{3},\quad\mbox{and}\quad s=3t,

the expression
3-h-3t
, given on the left hand side of
(\ref{Cdt=0})
, will be vanishing and then the condition will not be accomplished. To be able to handle this situation, we assume that
\eta
satisfies
\left|\eta\right|=\varsigma\,a^{-2}
, with
\varsigma<1
.

5.3.
Proof of Proposition
2.4
.

Here, we present the estimation for the scattering coefficient.
Similar to Lemma
5.1
, we first give the following formulation before proving Proposition
2.4
.

Lemma 5.3
.

Recall that
e_{n}^{(3)},n=1,2...,
are the eigenfunctions of
\nabla M
in the subspace
\nabla\mathcal{H}armonic
. Then we have

(5.56)

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}e^{(3)}_{n}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\Bigg[\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)+\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e^{(3)}_{n}(y)}{\left\|\cdot-y\right\|}\,dy\Bigg]+\overset{(3)}{R_{n}}.

where

\displaystyle\overset{(3)}{R_{n}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\,\lambda^{(3)}_{n}\right)}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\Bigg[\frac{\pm\,c_{0}\,a^{h}}{2\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n_{0}}}N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)+\frac{\pm\,c_{0}\,a^{h}}{2\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n_{0}}}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e^{(3)}_{n}(y)}{\left\|\cdot-y\right\|}\,dy+\frac{i\eta\,k^{3}a^{3}}{6\pi}\int_{B}e^{(3)}_{n}(y)\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{4\pi}\sum_{\ell\geq 3}\frac{(ika)^{\ell+1}}{(\ell+1)!}\int_{B}Hess\left(\left\|\cdot-y\right\|^{\ell}\right)\cdot e^{(3)}_{n}(y)\,dy-\frac{\left(1\mp c_{0}\,a^{h}\right)}{4\pi\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n_{0}}}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\frac{(ika)^{\ell+1}}{(\ell+1)!}\int_{B}\left\|\cdot-y\right\|^{\ell}e^{(3)}_{n}(y)\,dy\Bigg].

Proof.

Let us compute
\bm{T}_{k\,a}\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)
as follows:

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{k\,a}\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(I+\eta\,\nabla M\right)\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)+\eta\,\left(\nabla M^{ka}-\nabla M\right)\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,N^{ka}\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)

(5.57)

\displaystyle=\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)+\eta\,\left[\left(\nabla M^{ka}-\nabla M\right)-k^{2}a^{2}N^{ka}\right]\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right).

By combining with (
2.8
) and (
2.9
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\left[\left(\nabla M^{ka}-\nabla M\right)-k^{2}a^{2}N^{ka}\right]\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}N\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)-\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)}{\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{2}}\,dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{ik^{3}a^{3}}{6\pi}\int_{B}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy-\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}Hess\left(\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}\right)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 1}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy,

and therefore we see that (
5.3
) can be further written as

(5.58)

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{k\,a}\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)-\eta\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}N\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)-\eta\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)}{\lVert x-y\rVert^{2}}\,dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{i\eta k^{3}a^{3}}{6\pi}\int_{B}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy-\frac{\eta}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}\underset{x}{Hess}\left(\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}\right)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\eta k^{2}a^{2}}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 1}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy.

Taking the inverse of
\bm{T}_{k\,a}
on the both sides of (
5.58
), we obtain that

(5.59)

\displaystyle e_{n}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)-\eta\,\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}N\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\eta\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)}{\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{2}}\,dy-\frac{i\eta k^{3}a^{3}}{6\pi}\int_{B}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy\,\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(1\right)\,

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{4\pi}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}\underset{y}{Hess}\left(\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}\right)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\eta k^{2}a^{2}}{4\pi}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\sum_{n\geq 1}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy.

Dividing by
\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)
on the both sides of (
5.59
) and after rearranging terms, we derive that

(5.60)

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}e_{n}^{(3)}+\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\eta\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}N\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\eta\frac{k^{2}a^{2}}{2}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)}{\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{2}}\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\frac{i\eta k^{3}a^{3}}{6\pi}\int_{B}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy\,\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{4\pi\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}Hess\left(\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}\right)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\eta k^{2}a^{2}}{4\pi\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\sum_{n\geq 1}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy.

Since from (
2.11
), there holds

\eta\,k^{2}\,a^{2}=\frac{1\mp c_{0}\,a^{h}}{\lambda_{n_{0}}^{(1)}},

then by denoting

\displaystyle R_{n}^{(3)}:

\displaystyle=\frac{\mp\,c_{0}\,a^{h}}{2\lambda_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}N\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)+\frac{\mp\,c_{0}\,a^{h}}{2\lambda_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)}{\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{2}}\,dy

\displaystyle+\frac{i\eta k^{3}a^{3}}{6\pi\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\int_{B}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy\,\,\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{4\pi\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}Hess\left(\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}\right)\cdot e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(1\mp\,c_{0}\,a^{h}\right)}{4\pi\,\lambda_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\,\left(1+\eta\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\sum_{n\geq 1}\frac{(ika)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\int_{B}\lVert\cdot-y\rVert^{n}e_{n}^{(3)}(y)\,dy,

we can deduce (
5.56
) from (
5.60
), which completes the proof.
∎

We are now in a position to prove Proposition
2.4
as follows.

Proof of Proposition
2.4
.

We first give the estimation for the scattering coefficient
\mathcal{C}
.
Recall the definition of
\mathcal{C}
given by (
2.17
). After scaling to the domain
B
, we obtain

\mathcal{C}=a^{4}\,\int_{B}\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0)\right)(x)\,dx.

By expanding over the basis and using the fact that
\int_{B}e^{(1,2)}_{n}(x)dx=0
, we get

(5.61)

\mathcal{C}=a^{4}\,\sum_{n}\,\langle\bm{T}_{k\,a}^{-1}\left(\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0)\right),e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\,\int_{B}e^{(3)}_{n}(x)\,dx=a^{4}\,\sum_{n}\,\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\rangle\,\int_{B}e^{(3)}_{n}(x)\,dx.

From the expression (
5.56
) in Lemma
5.3
, in order to extract the dominant term of
(\ref{AI3})
, we need to analyse the term
S:=\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)
. For this we have

\displaystyle S

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}\left[\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right)+\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right)+\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right)\right],

\displaystyle S

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right),e^{(1)}_{\ell}\rangle\,\,\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}\left[e^{(1)}_{\ell}\right]+\sum_{\ell}\langle\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right),e^{(2)}_{\ell}\rangle\,\,\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}\left[e^{(2)}_{\ell}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\langle\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right),e^{(3)}_{\ell}\rangle\,\,\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}\left[e^{(3)}_{\ell}\right].

Now we use the result given in Lemma
5.1
and the fact that
\bm{T}_{k\,a}
, when restricted to
\mathbb{H}_{0}\left(Curl=0\right)
, is equal to
(1+\eta)\,I
to deduce that

\displaystyle S

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right),e^{(1)}_{\ell}\rangle\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{\ell}^{(1)}\right)}e_{\ell}^{(1)}+R_{\ell}\right)+\left(1+\eta\right)^{-1}\;\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\langle\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right),e^{(3)}_{\ell}\rangle\,\,\bm{T}_{-\,k\,a}^{-1}\left[e^{(3)}_{\ell}\right].

For the last term, we repeat the same computations as those done in Lemma
5.3
to deduce that

\sim\sum_{\ell}\langle\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right),e^{(3)}_{\ell}\rangle\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\lambda_{\ell}^{(3)}\right)}\,e_{\ell}^{(3)}.

Finally, the dominant term of
S
is given by

(5.62)

S\sim\sum_{\ell}\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e^{(3)}_{n}\right)\right),e^{(1)}_{\ell}\rangle\frac{1}{\left(1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{\ell}^{(1)}\right)}e_{\ell}^{(1)}.

Since

\bm{T}_{-k\,a}^{-1}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot e^{(3)}_{n}(y)}{\left\|\cdot-y\right\|}\,dy

has the same behaviour as
S
, the corresponding dominant term is also given by
(\ref{AI6})
.

Using
(\ref{AI3})
and
(\ref{AI6})
, the equation
(\ref{DJ.H})
takes the following form

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle a^{4}\,\sum_{n}\,\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\,\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e^{(3)}_{n}\rangle\,\int_{B}e^{(3)}_{n}(x)\,dx

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{4}}{2\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n_{0}}}\,\sum_{n}\,\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\,\sum_{\ell}\frac{\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)\right),e^{(1)}_{\ell}\rangle}{\left(1-k^{2}\,a^{2}\,\eta\,\lambda^{(1)}_{\ell}\right)}\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e^{(1)}_{\ell}\rangle\,\int_{B}e^{(3)}_{n}(x)\,dx.

Remark that the second term, when taking the index
\ell=n_{0}
, will dominate the first one. Consequently,

(5.63)

\mathcal{C}\sim a^{4-h}\,\sum_{n}\,\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\,\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(N\left(e_{n}^{(3)}\right)\right),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e^{(1)}_{n_{0}}\rangle\,\int_{B}e^{(3)}_{n}(x)\,dx=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{6-h}\right).

Next, we prove (
2.19
).
Recall that
W
solves (
2.16
).
By scaling (
2.16
) from
D
to
B
, we obtain that

(5.64)

\bm{T}_{-k\,a}\,\left(\widetilde{W}\right)(x)=a\,\mathcal{P}(x,0),\quad x\in B.

We project
\tilde{W}
onto the subspaces introduced in (
2.2
) as
\tilde{W}=\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)+\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)+\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right).

Then
(\ref{New-W-tilde})
, using the fact that
\nabla M^{k}\equiv 0
on
\mathbb{H}_{0}(\mathrm{div}\,=0)
and
(\ref{grad-M-k-2nd-subspace})
, becomes

(5.65)

\left(I-\,k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}N\right)\left(\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right)(x)+(1+\eta)\;\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(x)+\left(I+\eta\,\nabla M\right)\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right)(x)=a\,\mathcal{P}(x,0)-T(x),

where

T(x):=\eta\,\left(\nabla M^{-ka}-\nabla M\right)\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right)(x)-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\left(N^{-ka}\right)\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right)(x)-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\left(N^{-ka}-N\right)\left(\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right)(x),

which can be further rewritten as

(5.66)

\displaystyle T(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\eta\,\frac{(ka)^{2}}{2}N\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right)(x)+i\eta\,\frac{(ka)^{3}}{6\,\pi}\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)\,dy-\eta\,\frac{(ka)^{2}}{2}\int_{B}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\frac{A(x,y)\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)}{\left\|x-y\right\|^{2}}dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\,\eta\,\sum_{\ell\geq 3}\left(-ika\right)^{\ell+1}\int_{B}\frac{Hess\left(\left|x-y\right|^{\ell}\right)}{(\ell+1)!}\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)\,dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}}{4\pi}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left(-ika\right)^{\ell+1}\int_{B}\frac{\left|x-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\frac{1}{4\pi}\,\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left(-ika\right)^{\ell+1}\int_{B}\frac{\left|x-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy,

by using
(\ref{expansion-gradMk})
and
(\ref{expansion-Nk})
. Taking the inner product of
(\ref{Equa-Est-W})
with respect to
\left(e_{n}^{(1)}\right)
, we obtain

(5.67)

\langle\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle=\frac{1}{\left(1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n}\right)}\left[a\,\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle-\langle T,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right].

By taking the squared modulus and summing with respect to the index
n
, we deduce that

(5.68)

\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\,\frac{1}{\left|1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n}\right|^{2}}\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{-2h}\,\sum_{n}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

We need to compute
\underset{n}{\sum}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}
. Indeed,

\displaystyle\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\langle\,\int_{B}\,\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)}{\left\|x-y\right\|^{2}}dy,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{4}\sum_{\ell\geq 3}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{Hess\left(\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}\right)}{(\ell+1)!}\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)\,dy,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{6}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{6}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

Then,

(5.69)

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{B}\,\Phi_{0}(\cdot,y)\frac{A(\cdot,y)\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)}{\left\|x-y\right\|^{2}}dy\right\|^{2}+a^{4}\sum_{\ell\geq 3}\left\|\int_{B}\frac{Hess\left(\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}\right)}{(\ell+1)!}\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)\,dy\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{6}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left\|\int_{B}\frac{\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy\right\|^{2}+a^{6}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left\|\int_{B}\frac{\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{6}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Equation
(\ref{P1W})
takes the following form

(5.70)

\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\,\frac{1}{\left|1-k^{2}\,\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda^{(1)}_{n}\right|^{2}}\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{-2h}\,\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}.

We compute
\left\|\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}
in a similar manner. First, we take the inner product on the both sides of
(\ref{Equa-Est-W})
with respect to
\left(e_{n}^{(2)}\right)_{n}
, to obtain
\left(1+\eta\right)\,\,\langle\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right),e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle=\left[a\,\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle-\langle T,e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle\right]
. After taking the squared modulus and the series with respect to
n
, we obtain:

(5.71)

\left\|\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\left|1+\eta\right|^{2}}\;a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\,\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{4}\,\sum_{n}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

The computation of
\underset{n}{\sum}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle\right|^{2}
can be made similar to the computation of
\underset{n}{\sum}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}
. Indeed, from
(\ref{T.en1.en2})
, we deduce

\sum_{n}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle\right|^{2}\lesssim\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{6}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Thus
(\ref{P2W})
takes the form

(5.72)

\left\|\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\left|1+\eta\right|^{2}}\;a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\,\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(2)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{4}\,\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{10}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Next, we estimate
\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}
. From (
5.65
), we have

\displaystyle\langle\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(1+\eta\,\lambda_{n}^{(3)}\right)}\left[a\,\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle-\langle T,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right],

\displaystyle\left|\langle\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\left[a^{2}\,\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}+\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}\right],

(5.73)

\displaystyle\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\;a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\,\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{4}\,\sum_{n}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

For
\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\langle N\left(\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{2}\left|\langle\int_{B}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)\,dy,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{4}\,\sum_{\ell\geq 3}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{Hess\left(\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}\right)}{(\ell+1)!}\cdot\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)\,dy,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{6}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{6}\sum_{\ell\geq 2}\left|\langle\int_{B}\frac{\left|\cdot-y\right|^{\ell}}{(\ell+1)!}\;\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)(y)dy,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

Taking the series with respect to
n
index and utilizing the continuity of the Newtonian potential operator, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\left|\langle T,e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{2}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{4}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{6}\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{6}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}+a^{6}\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2},

and therefore
(\ref{P3W})
becomes

(5.74)

\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\left|\eta\right|^{-2}\;a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\,\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}+a^{10}\,\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}.

Plugging
(\ref{Equa-230})
into
(\ref{Equa-226})
, we can deduce

\displaystyle\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\,\sum_{n}\frac{\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}}{\left|1-k^{2}\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{(1)}\right|^{2}}+a^{6-2h}\,\sum_{n}\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\,\frac{\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}}{\left|1-k^{2}\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n_{0}}^{(1)}\right|^{2}}+a^{2}\,\sum_{n\neq n_{0}}\frac{\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(1)}\rangle\right|^{2}}{\left|1-k^{2}\eta\,a^{2}\,\lambda_{n}^{(1)}\right|^{2}}+a^{6-2h}\,\sum_{n}\left|\langle\mathcal{P}(\cdot,0),e_{n}^{(3)}\rangle\right|^{2}.

This implies,

(5.75)

\left\|\overset{1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{1-h}\right).

Combining with
(\ref{fin-W-1})
and
(\ref{Equa-230})
, we deduce that

(5.76)

\left\|\overset{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{3}\right).

Similarly, from
(\ref{fin-W-1}),(\ref{fin-W-3})
and
(\ref{Equa-228})
, we derive that

(5.77)

\left\|\overset{3}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\tilde{W}\right)\right\|=\mathcal{O}\left(a^{3}\right).

∎

6.
Derivation of the linear algebraic systems stated in Section
3
.

6.1.
Proof of Proposition
3.1
.

We first give the proof of the invertibility condition for the general form of the linear algebraic systerm as follows.

Proof of Proposition
3.1
.

We rewrite the linear algebraic system (
3.1
) in the following matrix form

(6.1)

\left(\begin{array}[]{c}Q_{1}\\
Q_{2}\\
\vdots\\
Q_{\aleph}\end{array}\right)-\eta\left(\begin{array}[]{cccc}0&[P_{D_{1}}]\cdot\Upsilon_{12}^{k}&\cdots&[P_{D_{1}}]\cdot\Upsilon_{1\aleph}^{k}\\
\left[P_{D_{2}}\right]\cdot\Upsilon_{21}^{k}&0&\cdots&\left[P_{D_{2}}\right]\cdot\Upsilon_{2\aleph}^{k}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\left[P_{D_{\aleph}}\right]\cdot\Upsilon_{\aleph 1}^{k}&\left[P_{D_{\aleph}}\right]\cdot\Upsilon_{\aleph 2}^{k}&\cdots&0\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}Q_{1}\\
Q_{2}\\
\vdots\\
Q_{\aleph}\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}J_{1}\\
J_{2}\\
\vdots\\
J_{\aleph}\end{array}\right),

where
\Upsilon_{ij}^{k}:=\Upsilon_{k}(z_{i},z_{j})
and we set
\left\rVert[P_{0}]\right\rVert:=\underset{j=1,...,\aleph}{\max}\left\lVert[P_{D_{j}}]\right\rVert_{\mathbb{L}^{\infty}(\Omega)}
.

Taking the inner product on the both sides of (
6.1
) with the following vector

\left(\left[P_{D_{1}}\right]\cdot Q_{1},\left[P_{D_{2}}\right]\cdot Q_{2},\cdots,\\
\left[P_{D_{\aleph}}\right]\cdot Q_{\aleph}\right)^{T},

by direct computations, we obtain from (
6.1
) that

(6.2)

\sum_{j=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{j}}]\cdot Q_{j}\right\rangle-\eta\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{k}\cdot Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot Q_{\ell}\right\rangle=\sum_{j=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle J_{j},[P_{D_{j}}]\cdot Q_{j}\right\rangle.

We rewrite (
6.2
) as follows

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle Q_{j},[P_{D_{j}}]\cdot Q_{j}\right\rangle-\eta\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0}\cdot Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot Q_{\ell}\right\rangle

(6.3)

\displaystyle-\eta\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}(\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{k}-\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0})\cdot Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot Q_{\ell}\right\rangle=\sum_{j=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle J_{j},[P_{D_{j}}]\cdot Q_{j}\right\rangle.

Denote

\Gamma^{0}:=\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0}\cdot Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot Q_{\ell}\right\rangle.

Since
\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0}
is harmonic, by the Mean Value Theorem we know that

\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0}:=\Upsilon^{0}(z_{\ell},z_{j})=\frac{1}{|\mathbf{B}_{\ell}|}\frac{1}{|\mathbf{B}_{j}|}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\,dx\,dz,

where
\mathbf{B}_{\ell}
and
\mathbf{B}_{j}
are two balls of radius
\dfrac{d}{2}
, centered at
z_{\ell}
and
z_{j}
, respectively.
Then

\displaystyle\Gamma^{0}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{|\mathbf{B}_{\ell}|}\frac{1}{|\mathbf{B}_{j}|}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\,dx\,dz\cdot Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot Q_{\ell}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{36}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\,dx\,dz\cdot Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot Q_{\ell}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{36}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\cdot Q_{j},\ [P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot Q_{\ell}\right\rangle\,dx\,dz.

We denote

(6.4)

V_{m}:=[P_{D_{m}}]\cdot Q_{m}\quad\mbox{for}\quad m=1,2,\cdots,\aleph,

and for
m=1,\cdots,\aleph
,

(6.5)

\Pi(x):=\begin{cases}V_{m}&\mbox{in}\quad\mathbf{B}_{m}\\
0&\mbox{otherwise}\end{cases},\ \Lambda(x):=\begin{cases}Q_{m}&\mbox{in}\quad\mathbf{B}_{m}\\
0&\mbox{otherwise}\end{cases},\ [\mathbf{P}]:=\begin{cases}[P_{D_{m}}]&\mbox{in}\quad\mathbf{B}_{m}\\
0&\mbox{otherwise}\end{cases}.

Set
\Omega:=\overset{\aleph}{\underset{m=1}{\cup}}\mathbf{B}_{m}
, then
\Gamma^{0}
becomes

\Gamma^{0}=\frac{36}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\left\langle[\mathbf{P}]\cdot\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\cdot\Lambda(x),\Pi(z)\right\rangle\,dx\,dz-\frac{36}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\left\langle[P_{D_{m}}]\cdot\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\cdot Q_{m},V_{m}\right\rangle\,dx\,dz,

It is direct to see that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\,dz\,dx=\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\underset{z}{Hess}\Phi_{0}(x,z)\,dz\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\underset{x}{\nabla}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\underset{z}{\nabla}\Phi_{0}(x,z)\,dz\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\underset{x}{Hess}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\Phi_{0}(x,z)\,dz\,dx.

From
[
23
, eq. (1.12)]
, we know that for
\mathbf{B}_{m}
, there holds:

(6.6)

N_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}(1)(x)=\frac{d^{2}}{8}-\frac{\left|x\right|^{2}}{6}\quad\text{henceforth}\quad Hess\,N_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}(1)(x)=-\frac{1}{3}I,\quad x\in\mathbf{B_{m}}.

Then,

(6.7)

\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\,dz\,dx=\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\underset{x}{Hess}N(1)(x)\,dx=-\frac{\pi d^{3}}{18}I.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\Gamma^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{36}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\left\langle[\mathbf{P}]\cdot\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\cdot\Lambda(x),\Pi(z)\right\rangle\,dx\,dz-\frac{36}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{m}}]\cdot\left(-\frac{\pi d^{3}}{18}I\right)\cdot Q_{m},V_{m}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle=\frac{36}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\left\langle[\mathbf{P}]\cdot\Upsilon^{0}(x,z)\cdot\Lambda(x),\Pi(z)\right\rangle\,dx\,dz+\frac{2}{\pi d^{3}}\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}|V_{m}|^{2}.

Now we proceed to estimate the difference term by denoting

\mathcal{R}^{k}:=\Gamma^{k}-\Gamma^{0}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\left(\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{k}-\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0}\right)\cdot Q_{j},\ V_{\ell}\right\rangle.

Combining with (
2.7
), we derive by direct computations that

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{k}-\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0}

\displaystyle=\underset{x}{Hess}\Phi_{k}(z_{\ell},z_{j})-\underset{x}{Hess}\Phi_{0}(z_{\ell},z_{j})+k^{2}\Phi_{k}(z_{\ell},z_{j})I\quad(x\neq y)

\displaystyle=\frac{k^{2}}{2}\Phi_{0}(z_{\ell},z_{j})I-\frac{ik^{3}}{12\pi}I+\frac{k^{2}}{2}\Phi_{0}(z_{\ell},z_{j})\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})\otimes(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{\lVert z_{\ell}-z_{j}\rVert^{2}}

(6.8)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}Hess\left(\lVert z_{\ell}-z_{j}\rVert^{n}\right)+k^{2}\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 0}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|^{n}.

Then
\mathcal{R}^{k}
becomes

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\left(\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{k}-\Upsilon_{\ell j}^{0}\right)\cdot Q_{j},\ V_{\ell}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\left(-\frac{ik^{3}}{12\pi}I\right)\cdot Q_{j},\ V_{\ell}\right\rangle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\left(\frac{k^{2}}{2}\frac{1}{4\pi|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|}I\right)\cdot Q_{j},\ V_{\ell}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\left(\frac{k^{2}}{2}\frac{1}{4\pi|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|}\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|}\otimes\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|}\right)\cdot Q_{j},\ V_{\ell}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\left(\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}Hess\left(\lVert z_{\ell}-z_{j}\rVert^{n}\right)\right)\cdot Q_{j},\ V_{\ell}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\left(\frac{k^{2}}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 0}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}\big|z_{\ell}-z_{j}\big|^{n}\right)\cdot Q_{j},\ V_{\ell}\right\rangle.

With the notation (
6.4
), we further derive

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{k}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{ik^{3}}{12\pi}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{k^{2}}{2}\frac{1}{4\pi|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{k^{2}}{2}\frac{1}{4\pi|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|}\left\langle\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|},Q_{j}\right\rangle\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|},V_{\ell}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}Hess(\lVert z_{\ell}-z_{j}\rVert^{n})\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{k^{2}}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 0}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|^{n}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle.

Since by the Mean Value Theorem, there holds that

\frac{1}{4\pi|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|}=\frac{1}{|\mathbf{B}_{\ell}|}\frac{1}{|\mathbf{B}_{j}|}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\,dx\,dy=\frac{36}{\pi^{2}\,d^{6}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\,dx\,dy,

then

(6.9)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{ik^{3}}{12\pi}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle+\frac{18k^{2}}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{18k^{2}}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\left\langle\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|},\ Q_{j}\right\rangle\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|},V_{\ell}\right\rangle\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}Hess(\lVert z_{\ell}-z_{j}\rVert^{n})\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{k^{2}}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 0}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|^{n}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle.

Considering the second term in (
6.9
), we know that

(6.10)

\displaystyle L_{6}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j=1\atop j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell,j=1}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle\,dx\,dy-\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\langle V_{m},V_{m}\rangle\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\Phi_{0}(x,y)[\mathbf{P}]\Lambda(x)\,dx\Pi(y)\,dy-\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\langle V_{m},V_{m}\rangle\,dx\,dy.

By utilizing (
6.6
) again, there holds

\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\,dx\,dy=\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}N(1)(y)\,dy=\int_{\mathbf{B}_{m}}\,\left(\frac{d^{2}}{8}-\frac{\left|y\right|^{2}}{6}\right)\,dy=\frac{\pi d^{5}}{60}.

Thus, (
6.10
) can be written as

L_{6}=\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\Phi_{0}(x,y)[\mathbf{P}]\Lambda(x)\Pi(y)\,dx\,dy-\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\frac{\pi d^{5}}{60}|V_{m}|^{2},

which implies that in (
6.9
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{k}

\displaystyle=-\frac{ik^{3}}{12\pi}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle+\frac{18k^{2}}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\Phi_{0}(x,y)[\mathbf{P}]\Lambda(x)\Pi(y)\,dx\,dy-\frac{3k^{2}}{10\pi d}\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}|V_{m}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{18k^{2}}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{\ell}}\int_{\mathbf{B}_{j}}\Phi_{0}(x,y)\left\langle\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|},\ Q_{j}\right\rangle\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]\cdot\frac{(z_{\ell}-z_{j})}{|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|},V_{\ell}\right\rangle\,dx\,dy

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{1}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 3}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}Hess(\lVert z_{\ell}-z_{j}\rVert^{n})\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle

(6.11)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\frac{k^{2}}{4\pi}\sum_{n\geq 0}\frac{(ik)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}|z_{\ell}-z_{j}|^{n}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle.

Now, we give the estimation of
\mathcal{R}^{k}
term by term.

•

Estimation of

\displaystyle L_{7,1}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-\frac{ik^{3}}{12\pi}\overset{\aleph}{\underset{\ell=1}{\sum}}\overset{\aleph}{\underset{j\neq\ell}{\sum}}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle,

(6.12)

\displaystyle\left|L_{7,1}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|-\frac{ik^{3}}{12\pi}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle\bigg|\lesssim k^{3}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}|\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},V_{\ell}\rangle|\lesssim k^{3}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\aleph}\sum_{j\neq\ell}^{\aleph}\bigg|\left\langle[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{j},[P_{D_{\ell}}]Q_{\ell}\right\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k^{3}\left\lVert[P_{0}]\right\rVert^{2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{\aleph}|Q_{j}|\right)^{2}\lesssim k^{3}\left\lVert[P_{0}]\right\rVert^{2}\,\aleph\,\sum_{j=1}^{\aleph}|Q_{j}|^{2}.

•

Estimation of

L_{7,2}:=\frac{18k^{2}}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\Phi_{0}(x,y)[\mathbf{P}]\Lambda(x)\,dx\Pi(y)\,dy.

Following the definition of the Newtonian potential operator, we have

(6.13)

\displaystyle\left|L_{7,2}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{18k^{2}}{\pi^{2}d^{6}}\int_{\Omega}\int_{\Omega}\Phi_{0}(x,y)[\mathbf{P}]\Lambda(x)\,dx\Pi(y)\,dy\bigg|\lesssim k^{2}d^{-6}\big|\int_{\Omega}N(\Pi)(x)\cdot[\mathbf{P}]\Lambda(x)\,dx\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k^{2}d^{-6}\lVert N(\Pi)\rVert_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\lVert[\mathbf{P}]\Lambda\rVert_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim k^{2}d^{-6}\lVert N\rVert_{\mathcal{L}(\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega);\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega))}\lVert\Pi\rVert_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\left\lVert[P_{0}]\right\rVert\,\lVert\Lambda\rVert_{\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k^{2}d^{-3}\lVert N\rVert_{\mathcal{L}(\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega);\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega))}\left(\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}|V_{m}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert[P_{0}]\right\rVert\,\left(\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}|Q_{m}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k^{2}d^{-3}\lVert N\rVert_{\mathcal{L}(\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega);\mathbb{L}^{2}(\Omega))}\left\lVert[P_{0}]\right\rVert^{2}\,\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\big|Q_{m}\big|^{2}.

•

Estimation of

\displaystyle L_{7,3}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-\frac{3k^{2}}{10\pi d}\overset{\aleph}{\underset{m=1}{\sum}}|V_{m}|^{2},

(6.14)

\displaystyle\left|L_{7,3}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k^{2}d^{-1}\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}|V_{m}|^{2}=k^{2}d^{-1}\sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\big|[P_{D_{m}}]Q_{m}\big|^{2}=k^{2}d^{-1}\,\left\|[P_{0}]\right\|^{2}\ \sum_{m=1}^{\aleph}\big|Q_{m}\big|^{2}.
